```text
MicroCOM II Clearly the industry's most advanced Communications System With its superior design and advanced PC based software, MicroCOM IL moves communications technology a whole generation forward, outperforming all the rest by significant margin . MicroCOM ILCQMMUNICATIONSSYSTEM
```
```text
INTRODUCTION
The Ward-Beck Systems MicroCOM II Communications system has evolved from over eighteen years of experience in the design and manufacturer of intercom radio and television broadcast facilities. This evolutionary process has assured the highest degree of system design relia bility along with the utilize- tion of the best of the ever changing technologies
From each M7880 series communications terminal only three pair of audio wires are required to connect it to the Micro- COM II Matrix (input, output and con- trol pairs).
Recovery from power interruption occurs in matter of seconds without the requirements of updating the system status from central storage mediums
The control and programming of the MicroCOM II system is based on multi-microprocessor and central microcomputer  design philosophy. The latest  high  speed complimentary MOS technology along with electrically eras- able memories results in system re - quiring minimal amount of power, and without the necessity for any back- up power requirements  Where it is desired to configure communications system with central programming   capabilities, an IBM-PC type personal   microcomputer may be used as Central Assignment Com- puter. This permits an operator to reprogram communications terminal control functions from central point: Specific crosspoints may also be set locally on any terminal to mcct the requirements of system setup at any time .
The system is capa ble of being con- figured to accommodate different operat- ing philosophies with little Or no hard- ware changes Existing systems opera ting in the field may be easily expanded or redefined with minimal interruption:
The central audio matrix is made up of onc Or more 24 input 24 output blocks_ These matrix blocks may be interconnected together to form a sys- tem as defined for particular set of requirements; Only the portion of the system presently utilized need be sup- plied. All  connections to and from the matrix blocks are via connectors  Input and output  jackfields as well as test monitor facilities may be included as part of a commun ica tions system_ Audio levels are normally set to +8 dBm 600 ohms.
Each output block of twenty-four matrix buses is under the control of microcontroller. Crosspoint control data is refreshed and  updated in the order of twenty times per second. The data transfer between each of the remote communication terminals and the matrix control system is via balanced bi-directional serial data line_ The overall control system is   designed such that it is not totally dependent on a single electronic module or building block (a philosophy of distributed con- trol). Thc system test facilities that are
certain setup of control   functions may be stored on floppy disk or new setup  transferred from disk   into the system The Central Assignment Computer has   only to be left on Iinc when ma king system changes Or per - forming any diagnostic type functions A new communications terminal if placed on line (connected to the central matrix) will automatically learn the current system assignment and control   setup without any special central assignment intervention.
```

```text
The central  switching matrix of the MicroCOM II Communications System is configured with modular  blocks, each with twenty-four balanced audio inputs and twenty-four balanced audio outputs These matrix blocks, each of which is housed in MF-TD mounting frame_ maybe interconnected in modular fashion to configure matrices to sizes as great as 960x960. 7.' .. ~C; Each of the 24x24 matrix building blocks may be broken down further to the module level of twenty-four inputs to one output:
not left in the system that do not 4 , have utilization for particular application. These unused crosspoints in some instances could detract from the overall system reliability and performance. ~icos` 'J? ` .L i ::. Only natural convective cooling is 77 required for the matrix: No fans or external forced air is required under normal circumstances:
Since all components of the matrix are modular in nature, system changes and additions  normally can be made with the system active and operational AIl audio inputs and outputs are actively balanced about ground, with an opera ting level of +8 dBm 600 ohms: io: TV0 Input buffer amplifiers (M770) provide either matched or bridging load to the input source Test select crosspoints are included integral to these amplifiers for the purpose of injecting  optional audio test signals along any input bus: The matrix output amplifiers have low impedance output, capable of driving a 600 ohm load up to a level of +20 dBm (little   degradation of level capability occurs when driving lower impedance loads) An optional monitor listen test bus is normally bridged across all matrix outputs:
The crosspoint module (M724B) consists of twen ty-four balanced solid state FET switches: This results in a switch design which has high bridging input impedance (40K ohms), cutoff of greater than 100 dB very low leakage or crosstalk, and low distortion in the On state.
This matrix philosophy of size and configuration capabilities has the follow- ing major advantages:
Only the portion of the matrix required for specific application need be installed.
Only the crosspoint modules within 24x24 assembly need be simple- mented with expansion capabilities being able to be realized at any time:
AlI matrix inputs and outputs may be normalled through jackfields of cus- tomers choice: This typically is the PJ339 or the miniature PJ839 (Bantam) series of tip; ring, sleeve type telephone jacks. The final terminating point may be  terminal blocks or communication terminals interfacing connectors (9 pin D type connectors) Typically three pair audio cable such as Belden #8777 used to interface communication terminal with the centraL matrix
```
